===== arc journal latest --help =====
Resolve the newest artifact filed under one topic and print it, so a workflow that appends continuations under a stable topic can read its own tail back without reproducing arc's ordering rules. The topic must match exactly; a similarly prefixed topic never matches. The hot journal wins over the cold archive whenever both hold a match. Inside one timestamp second a transition's terminal successor is preferred, and otherwise the order the event log recorded the artifacts in decides; a numeric filename suffix is never an ordering of its own. Records no events; ledger reads do not require a parseable append policy

Usage: arc journal latest [OPTIONS] <TOPIC>

Arguments:
  <TOPIC>  Kebab-case topic slug, matched exactly

Options:
      --actor <ACTOR>                Acting identity, from ARC_ACTOR when the flag is absent. Falls back to <harness>:<session> when both are known, else git user.name; arc records either as an identity nobody declared. A write refuses when none of these names one, and refuses a declared placeholder (unknown, none, n/a, ...) or a harness:model form carrying one; a harness:session form is an actor. A read proceeds without one
      --global                       Target the operator journal at <ai-home>/arc/journals/global, from any directory. All journal kinds share this scope. With promote, the source is a project memory and the destination is global
      --kind <KIND>                  Restrict to one artifact kind, including kinds no longer written
      --harness <HARNESS>            Harness label, e.g. claude, codex, opencode. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a harness nobody established [env: ARC_HARNESS=test]
      --json                         Emit the resolved identity alongside the body as JSON
      --session <SESSION>            Native session ID of the acting harness thread. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a session nobody established [env: ARC_SESSION=session-a]
      --session-link <SESSION_LINK>  Private web link for the acting session; recorded only in arc events [env: ARC_SESSION_LINK=]
      --model <MODEL>                Declare a model slug with optional #effort. Without this flag or ARC_MODEL, each invocation resolves the acting session store once when it writes. A corroborated Claude recording without a model selection is re-read every 50 ms for up to 1 second; other store answers return immediately. Declarations retain any observed disagreement and its coordinate. Model and supplied effort segments must be nonempty. A write refuses a placeholder model or effort (unknown, none, n/a, ...) and a session id as the model, whether flagged or in ARC_MODEL: drop the declaration and arc records the observed model
      --on-behalf-of <ON_BEHALF_OF>  Subject a lead runs delegated ceremony for; recorded beside the invoker. A write refuses a placeholder or a harness:model form carrying one [env: ARC_ON_BEHALF_OF=]
      --role <ROLE>                  Execution boundary: implementer | reviewer | lead [env: ARC_ROLE=]
      --change <change_flag>         Change to act on, wherever the positional is optional
      --sandbox <SANDBOX>            Absolute prefix that stands in for the home directory: every root arc writes by default (ledger under a data root, journal, registry, configuration, worktrees, temp) lives beneath it [env: ARC_SANDBOX=<sandbox>]
  -h, --help                         Print help

===== arc journal discussion --help =====
Derived summary of a discussion: stance tally, participants, replies, age, and resolution with a resolver-participation flag (read-only). The tally counts `Position: for | against | amend` lines inside `### Position` blocks, and flags blocks that state no stance. Indented code and unrecorded fenced entry examples are prose; recorded position, evidence, question, and answer markers terminate an open fence, and a heading repeating an entry ID already met is a quotation. Reports each open question with the positions argued under every option, so a branch nobody explored is visible before the question is answered. Resolve a discussion with `journal consume --outcome done --decision <file>` Records no events; ledger reads do not require a parseable append policy. Recorded models that are missing, placeholders or session ids print as model not recorded, qualified by the harness when it is established. JSON production identities retain raw models with model_recorded: false when missing, placeholders or session ids; valid models omit the flag

Usage: arc journal discussion [OPTIONS] <FILENAME>

Arguments:
  <FILENAME>  Discussion artifact filename inside the journal dir (a name, not a path)

Options:
      --actor <ACTOR>                Acting identity, from ARC_ACTOR when the flag is absent. Falls back to <harness>:<session> when both are known, else git user.name; arc records either as an identity nobody declared. A write refuses when none of these names one, and refuses a declared placeholder (unknown, none, n/a, ...) or a harness:model form carrying one; a harness:session form is an actor. A read proceeds without one
      --global                       Target the operator journal at <ai-home>/arc/journals/global, from any directory. All journal kinds share this scope. With promote, the source is a project memory and the destination is global
      --json                         Emit structured JSON instead of text
      --harness <HARNESS>            Harness label, e.g. claude, codex, opencode. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a harness nobody established [env: ARC_HARNESS=test]
      --session <SESSION>            Native session ID of the acting harness thread. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a session nobody established [env: ARC_SESSION=session-a]
      --session-link <SESSION_LINK>  Private web link for the acting session; recorded only in arc events [env: ARC_SESSION_LINK=]
      --model <MODEL>                Declare a model slug with optional #effort. Without this flag or ARC_MODEL, each invocation resolves the acting session store once when it writes. A corroborated Claude recording without a model selection is re-read every 50 ms for up to 1 second; other store answers return immediately. Declarations retain any observed disagreement and its coordinate. Model and supplied effort segments must be nonempty. A write refuses a placeholder model or effort (unknown, none, n/a, ...) and a session id as the model, whether flagged or in ARC_MODEL: drop the declaration and arc records the observed model
      --on-behalf-of <ON_BEHALF_OF>  Subject a lead runs delegated ceremony for; recorded beside the invoker. A write refuses a placeholder or a harness:model form carrying one [env: ARC_ON_BEHALF_OF=]
      --role <ROLE>                  Execution boundary: implementer | reviewer | lead [env: ARC_ROLE=]
      --change <change_flag>         Change to act on, wherever the positional is optional
      --sandbox <SANDBOX>            Absolute prefix that stands in for the home directory: every root arc writes by default (ledger under a data root, journal, registry, configuration, worktrees, temp) lives beneath it [env: ARC_SANDBOX=<sandbox>]
  -h, --help                         Print help

===== arc journal transition --help =====
Change a live artifact's workflow kind without rewriting history: a typed successor is written, linked back with `supersedes`, and the source is retired as superseded. The safe manual sequence, performed as one guarded operation rather than hand-composed by every caller. Promotion to a code change stays with `begin --from-journal`, which is implementation authorization and never a kind conversion. The complete body is staged durably before the relation is recorded and published exclusively. Reusing a matching preparation syncs its file and directory before recording the relation. Retry uses those staged bytes. Plan targets require valid inherited planner metadata or --no-planner. A legacy successor requires verification against the original body and title; a mismatch preserves the artifact and refuses retirement. A successor whose publication evidence is incomplete is a pending transition and is refused as a source; retry the transition that created it

Usage: arc journal transition [OPTIONS] --to <TO> <FILENAME>

Arguments:
  <FILENAME>  Artifact filename inside the journal dir (a name, not a path)

Options:
      --actor <ACTOR>
          Acting identity, from ARC_ACTOR when the flag is absent. Falls back to <harness>:<session> when both are known, else git user.name; arc records either as an identity nobody declared. A write refuses when none of these names one, and refuses a declared placeholder (unknown, none, n/a, ...) or a harness:model form carrying one; a harness:session form is an actor. A read proceeds without one
      --global
          Target the operator journal at <ai-home>/arc/journals/global, from any directory. All journal kinds share this scope. With promote, the source is a project memory and the destination is global
      --to <TO>
          The successor's kind [possible values: note, memory, plan, handoff, review, conclusion, todo, later, discussion, decision, incident, feature-request]
      --body-file <BODY_FILE>
          Body source for the successor: a file path, or '-' for stdin. Omitted, the successor carries the source body verbatim under a heading naming what changed
      --harness <HARNESS>
          Harness label, e.g. claude, codex, opencode. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a harness nobody established [env: ARC_HARNESS=test]
      --session <SESSION>
          Native session ID of the acting harness thread. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a session nobody established [env: ARC_SESSION=session-a]
      --title <TITLE>
          Title for the `# <title>` heading; a body opening on prose is otherwise headed from the topic slug
      --reason <REASON>
          Why the kind is changing, recorded in the transition event
      --session-link <SESSION_LINK>
          Private web link for the acting session; recorded only in arc events [env: ARC_SESSION_LINK=]
      --dry-run
          Print the successor and the lifecycle effects without writing anything
      --model <MODEL>
          Declare a model slug with optional #effort. Without this flag or ARC_MODEL, each invocation resolves the acting session store once when it writes. A corroborated Claude recording without a model selection is re-read every 50 ms for up to 1 second; other store answers return immediately. Declarations retain any observed disagreement and its coordinate. Model and supplied effort segments must be nonempty. A write refuses a placeholder model or effort (unknown, none, n/a, ...) and a session id as the model, whether flagged or in ARC_MODEL: drop the declaration and arc records the observed model
      --acknowledge-claim <ACKNOWLEDGE_CLAIM>
          Acknowledge one open claim on the source artifact by ID (repeatable). A kind change retires the source, ending work in progress on it
      --on-behalf-of <ON_BEHALF_OF>
          Subject a lead runs delegated ceremony for; recorded beside the invoker. A write refuses a placeholder or a harness:model form carrying one [env: ARC_ON_BEHALF_OF=]
      --planned-by <PLANNED_BY>
          Assert an additional material planner on a plan successor (repeatable): a one-line JSON object in the shape `arc journal plan --help` states for --planned-by. A planner the successor would record, asserted or inherited, that carries a placeholder or a session id model refuses, under --dry-run too; correct an inherited one with `arc journal correct <file> --target artifact --field planners`, or pass --no-planner
      --role <ROLE>
          Execution boundary: implementer | reviewer | lead [env: ARC_ROLE=]
      --change <change_flag>
          Change to act on, wherever the positional is optional
      --no-planner
          Preserve unknown authorship on a plan successor
      --sandbox <SANDBOX>
          Absolute prefix that stands in for the home directory: every root arc writes by default (ledger under a data root, journal, registry, configuration, worktrees, temp) lives beneath it [env: ARC_SANDBOX=<sandbox>]
  -h, --help
          Print help

===== arc journal promote --help =====
Promote a live project memory into the operator journal. Requires --global. Both journals record the relation; the project memory is consumed as superseded and its historical body is retained. Both journals must have settled exchanges. An interrupted promotion is completed before subsequent writes or by retrying the same command. Complete staged bodies publish exclusively; success requires body and event replay verification. Changed bodies or a different global journal refuse recovery. Open claims require release-claim. Cleanup keeps global recovery discoverable until the source intent is durably removed. A committed relation with a remaining source intent is verified in hot or archived storage and needs only metadata cleanup

Usage: arc journal promote [OPTIONS] <FILENAME>

Arguments:
  <FILENAME>  Project memory filename (a name, not a path)

Options:
      --actor <ACTOR>                Acting identity, from ARC_ACTOR when the flag is absent. Falls back to <harness>:<session> when both are known, else git user.name; arc records either as an identity nobody declared.       --harness <HARNESS>            Harness label, e.g. claude, codex, opencode. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a harness nobody established [env: ARC_HARNESS=test]
      --session <SESSION>            Native session ID of the acting harness thread. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a session nobody established [env: ARC_SESSION=session-a]
      --session-link <SESSION_LINK>  Private web link for the acting session; recorded only in arc events [env: ARC_SESSION_LINK=]
      --model <MODEL>                Declare a model slug with optional #effort. Without this flag or ARC_MODEL, each invocation resolves the acting session store once when it writes. ===== arc journal export --help =====
Export selected journal artifacts as a versioned bundle for another replica. --global exports the operator journal without Git pairing. The bundle carries each artifact body, every log record written about it as written, including records this build cannot read and records state replay sets aside, its body digest, the exporting replica, and every artifact the selection references by filename. Exports arc-journal-bundle/2. Checksums retain event field order and opaque payload tokens, excluding formatting whitespace. Records no events; ledger reads do not require a parseable append policy

Usage: arc journal export [OPTIONS] --output <OUTPUT> <FILES>...

Arguments:
  <FILES>...  Artifact filenames inside the journal dir (names, not paths); the dependency closure of what they reference travels with them

Options:
      --actor <ACTOR>                Acting identity, from ARC_ACTOR when the flag is absent. Falls back to <harness>:<session> when both are known, else git user.name; arc records either as an identity nobody declared. A read proceeds without one
      --global                       Target the operator journal at <ai-home>/arc/journals/global, from any directory. All journal kinds share this scope. With promote, the source is a project memory and the destination is global
      --output <OUTPUT>              Output file ('-' for stdout)
      --harness <HARNESS>            Harness label, e.g. claude, codex, opencode. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a harness nobody established [env: ARC_HARNESS=test]
      --session <SESSION>            Native session ID of the acting harness thread. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a session nobody established [env: ARC_SESSION=session-a]

===== arc journal import --help =====
Import arc-journal-bundle/2 or /1 from another paired replica. --global accepts only operator journal bundles, without Git pairing. Prefix revisions retain the longer body. Concurrent appended sections merge by event identity, ordered by timestamp then ID. Answers to a reopened question retain distinct append identities. Edits to the base or a shared block refuse the entire bundle, naming both digests and the first differing block. Storage tiers must agree. Checksums retain event field order and opaque payload tokens, excluding formatting whitespace. Artifacts carrying unsupported events are held back as a unit, reported with the event kind and schema; their dependent artifacts are held back too. Admitted artifacts retain their provenance. Receipts cover only admitted artifacts, so held-back artifacts are retried on every import. Deduplication includes understood physical log records rejected by state replay. Events replay at their receiving append positions. A completely admitted bundle changes nothing on a repeated import. Interrupted writes retain a prepared transaction; an import completes it before comparing revisions. Every journal write, including provenance repair and spool promotion, refuses a pending import and names its bundle digest and the remedy: `arc journal import <bundle>`, or `arc journal --global import <bundle>` when the global journal holds it. Reads stay allowed. Recovery refuses to overwrite unexpected body or event-log changes. --dry-run requires a completed exchange

Usage: arc journal import [OPTIONS] <INPUT>

Arguments:
  <INPUT>  Input file ('-' for stdin)

Options:
      --actor <ACTOR>                Acting identity, from ARC_ACTOR when the flag is absent. Falls back to <harness>:<session> when both are known, else git user.name; arc records either as an identity nobody declared. A write refuses when none of these names one, and refuses a declared placeholder (unknown, none, n/a, ...) or a harness:model form carrying one; a harness:session form is an actor. A read proceeds without one
      --dry-run                      Validate and report without writing artifacts, events, or a receipt
      --global                       Target the operator journal at <ai-home>/arc/journals/global, from any directory. All journal kinds share this scope. With promote, the source is a project memory and the destination is global
      --harness <HARNESS>            Harness label, e.g. claude, codex, opencode. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a harness nobody established [env: ARC_HARNESS=test]
      --session <SESSION>            Native session ID of the acting harness thread. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a session nobody established [env: ARC_SESSION=session-a]
      --session-link <SESSION_LINK>  Private web link for the acting session; recorded only in arc events [env: ARC_SESSION_LINK=]
      --model <MODEL>                Declare a model slug with optional #effort. Without this flag or ARC_MODEL, each invocation resolves the acting session store once when it writes. A corroborated Claude recording without a model selection is re-read every 50 ms for up to 1 second; other store answers return immediately. Declarations retain any observed disagreement and its coordinate. Model and supplied effort segments must be nonempty. A write refuses a placeholder model or effort (unknown, none, n/a, ...) and a session id as the model, whether flagged or in ARC_MODEL: drop the declaration and arc records the observed model
      --on-behalf-of <ON_BEHALF_OF>  Subject a lead runs delegated ceremony for; recorded beside the invoker. A write refuses a placeholder or a harness:model form carrying one [env: ARC_ON_BEHALF_OF=]
      --role <ROLE>                  Execution boundary: implementer | reviewer | lead [env: ARC_ROLE=]
      --change <change_flag>         Change to act on, wherever the positional is optional
      --sandbox <SANDBOX>            Absolute prefix that stands in for the home directory: every root arc writes by default (ledger under a data root, journal, registry, configuration, worktrees, temp) lives beneath it [env: ARC_SANDBOX=<sandbox>]
  -h, --help                         Print help

===== arc replica provisional resolve --help =====
Resolve late delivery against this change's accepting integration. Requires current authority. Forge obligations require asserted integration with the exact external reference

Usage: arc replica provisional resolve [OPTIONS] --integration <INTEGRATION> --because <BECAUSE> <CHANGE>

Arguments:
  <CHANGE>  Change whose provisional delivery is being reconciled

Options:
      --actor <ACTOR>                Acting identity, from ARC_ACTOR when the flag is absent. Falls back to <harness>:<session> when both are known, else git user.name; arc records either as an identity nobody declared. A write refuses when none of these names one, and refuses a declared placeholder (unknown, none, n/a, ...) or a harness:model form carrying one; a harness:session form is an actor. A read proceeds without one
      --integration <INTEGRATION>    Accepting change-integrated or integration-asserted event ID
      --harness <HARNESS>            Harness label, e.g. claude, codex, opencode. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a harness nobody established [env: ARC_HARNESS=test]
      --provisional <PROVISIONAL>    Obligation event IDs; omitted, resolve every matching outstanding obligation
      --because <BECAUSE>            Reason for explicit reconciliation; never silently disposes of a refusal
      --session <SESSION>            Native session ID of the acting harness thread. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a session nobody established [env: ARC_SESSION=session-a]
      --session-link <SESSION_LINK>  Private web link for the acting session; recorded only in arc events [env: ARC_SESSION_LINK=]

===== arc journal lane open --help =====
Open or replace an advisory work lane

A lane's owner is the declared harness and session together, so both must be given. Opening replaces only that owner's own previous lane; another harness presenting the same session string keeps its lane. Liveness follows the owner's latest recorded activity by timestamp, whatever order records reached the log in; a spooled write promoted late never lowers it. Ownership checks and lane changes share the journal transition lock.

Usage: arc journal lane open [OPTIONS] <TOPIC>

Arguments:
  <TOPIC>
          Kebab-case topic slug naming the lane

Options:
      --actor <ACTOR>
          Acting identity, from ARC_ACTOR when the flag is absent. Falls back to <harness>:<session> when both are known, else git user.name; arc records either as an identity nobody declared. A write refuses when none of these names one, and refuses a declared placeholder (unknown, none, n/a, ...) or a harness:model form carrying one; a harness:session form is an actor. A read proceeds without one

      --global
          Target the operator journal at <ai-home>/arc/journals/global, from any directory. All journal kinds share this scope. With promote, the source is a project memory and the destination is global

      --scope <SCOPE>
          Journal topics this lane covers, so `journal open` can annotate the items another session is already holding

      --harness <HARNESS>
          Harness label, e.g. claude, codex, opencode. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a harness nobody established
          
          [env: ARC_HARNESS=test]

      --ttl <TTL>
          How long the lane stays live without activity (e.g. 30m, 2h)
          
          [default: 2h]

      --session <SESSION>
          Native session ID of the acting harness thread. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a session nobody established
          
          [env: ARC_SESSION=session-a]

      --status <STATUS>
          What the lane is currently doing, shown beside it

      --session-link <SESSION_LINK>
          Private web link for the acting session; recorded only in arc events
          
          [env: ARC_SESSION_LINK=]

      --status-file <STATUS_FILE>
          Read the lane status from a file ('-' for stdin)

      --model <MODEL>
          Declare a model slug with optional #effort. Without this flag or ARC_MODEL, each invocation resolves the acting session store once when it writes. A corroborated Claude recording without a model selection is re-read every 50 ms for up to 1 second; other store answers return immediately. Declarations retain any observed disagreement and its coordinate. Model and supplied effort segments must be nonempty. A write refuses a placeholder model or effort (unknown, none, n/a, ...) and a session id as the model, whether flagged or in ARC_MODEL: drop the declaration and arc records the observed model

      --on-behalf-of <ON_BEHALF_OF>
          Subject a lead runs delegated ceremony for; recorded beside the invoker. A write refuses a placeholder or a harness:model form carrying one
          
          [env: ARC_ON_BEHALF_OF=]

      --role <ROLE>
          Execution boundary: implementer | reviewer | lead
          
          [env: ARC_ROLE=]

      --change <change_flag>
          Change to act on, wherever the positional is optional

      --sandbox <SANDBOX>
          Absolute prefix that stands in for the home directory: every root arc writes by default (ledger under a data root, journal, registry, configuration, worktrees, temp) lives beneath it
          
          [env: ARC_SANDBOX=<sandbox>]

  -h, --help
          Print help (see a summary with '-h')

===== arc journal lane renew --help =====
Renew a lane owned by this harness and session. Recorded renewal requires both owner coordinates and compares them verbatim. Lane existence and ownership are checked before status input and again under the transition lock; opening and closure share that lock

Usage: arc journal lane renew [OPTIONS] <TOPIC>

Arguments:
  <TOPIC>  The lane to renew

Options:
      --actor <ACTOR>                Acting identity, from ARC_ACTOR when the flag is absent. Falls back to <harness>:<session> when both are known, else git user.name; arc records either as an identity nobody declared. A write refuses when none of these names one, and refuses a declared placeholder (unknown, none, n/a, ...) or a harness:model form carrying one; a harness:session form is an actor. A read proceeds without one
      --global                       Target the operator journal at <ai-home>/arc/journals/global, from any directory. All journal kinds share this scope. With promote, the source is a project memory and the destination is global
      --ttl <TTL>                    Replace the liveness window (defaults to the lane's current one)
      --harness <HARNESS>            Harness label, e.g. claude, codex, opencode. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a harness nobody established [env: ARC_HARNESS=test]
      --status <STATUS>              Replace what the lane is currently doing
      --session <SESSION>            Native session ID of the acting harness thread. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a session nobody established [env: ARC_SESSION=session-a]
      --status-file <STATUS_FILE>    Read the lane status from a file ('-' for stdin)
      --session-link <SESSION_LINK>  Private web link for the acting session; recorded only in arc events [env: ARC_SESSION_LINK=]
      --model <MODEL>                Declare a model slug with optional #effort. Without this flag or ARC_MODEL, each invocation resolves the acting session store once when it writes. A corroborated Claude recording without a model selection is re-read every 50 ms for up to 1 second; other store answers return immediately. Declarations retain any observed disagreement and its coordinate. Model and supplied effort segments must be nonempty. A write refuses a placeholder model or effort (unknown, none, n/a, ...) and a session id as the model, whether flagged or in ARC_MODEL: drop the declaration and arc records the observed model
      --on-behalf-of <ON_BEHALF_OF>  Subject a lead runs delegated ceremony for; recorded beside the invoker. A write refuses a placeholder or a harness:model form carrying one [env: ARC_ON_BEHALF_OF=]
      --role <ROLE>                  Execution boundary: implementer | reviewer | lead [env: ARC_ROLE=]
      --change <change_flag>         Change to act on, wherever the positional is optional
      --sandbox <SANDBOX>            Absolute prefix that stands in for the home directory: every root arc writes by default (ledger under a data root, journal, registry, configuration, worktrees, temp) lives beneath it [env: ARC_SANDBOX=<sandbox>]
  -h, --help                         Print help

===== arc journal lane close --help =====
Close a lane

A live lane closes only for the owning harness and session; a stale one closes for anyone with `--outcome expired`. Recorded closure requires both owner coordinates; foreign expiry is checked at the event timestamp. Ownership checks and lane changes share the journal transition lock.

Usage: arc journal lane close [OPTIONS] <TOPIC>

Arguments:
  <TOPIC>
          The lane to close

Options:
      --actor <ACTOR>
          Acting identity, from ARC_ACTOR when the flag is absent. Falls back to <harness>:<session> when both are known, else git user.name; arc records either as an identity nobody declared. A write refuses when none of these names one, and refuses a declared placeholder (unknown, none, n/a, ...) or a harness:model form carrying one; a harness:session form is an actor. A read proceeds without one

      --global
          Target the operator journal at <ai-home>/arc/journals/global, from any directory. All journal kinds share this scope. With promote, the source is a project memory and the destination is global

      --outcome <OUTCOME>
          How the lane ended
          
          [default: done]
          [possible values: done, handoff, abandoned, expired]

      --harness <HARNESS>
          Harness label, e.g. claude, codex, opencode. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a harness nobody established
          
          [env: ARC_HARNESS=test]

      --note <NOTE>
          Free-text detail recorded with the closure

      --session <SESSION>
          Native session ID of the acting harness thread. A write refuses a placeholder such as unknown; omit a session nobody established
          
          [env: ARC_SESSION=session-a]

      --session-link <SESSION_LINK>
          Private web link for the acting session; recorded only in arc events
          
          [env: ARC_SESSION_LINK=]

      --model <MODEL>
          Declare a model slug with optional #effort. Without this flag or ARC_MODEL, each invocation resolves the acting session store once when it writes. A corroborated Claude recording without a model selection is re-read every 50 ms for up to 1 second; other store answers return immediately. Declarations retain any observed disagreement and its coordinate. Model and supplied effort segments must be nonempty. A write refuses a placeholder model or effort (unknown, none, n/a, ...) and a session id as the model, whether flagged or in ARC_MODEL: drop the declaration and arc records the observed model

      --on-behalf-of <ON_BEHALF_OF>
          Subject a lead runs delegated ceremony for; recorded beside the invoker. A write refuses a placeholder or a harness:model form carrying one
          
          [env: ARC_ON_BEHALF_OF=]

      --role <ROLE>
          Execution boundary: implementer | reviewer | lead
          
          [env: ARC_ROLE=]

      --change <change_flag>
          Change to act on, wherever the positional is optional

      --sandbox <SANDBOX>
          Absolute prefix that stands in for the home directory: every root arc writes by default (ledger under a data root, journal, registry, configuration, worktrees, temp) lives beneath it
          
          [env: ARC_SANDBOX=<sandbox>]

  -h, --help
          Print help (see a summary with '-h')
